Usage

```
{'build_pack_weights': 'pack ternary quantized weights into a compact 2-bit-per-value uint8 tensor format', 'build_unpack_weights': 'unpack a packed 2-bit-per-value uint8 tensor back into ternary weights with a specified dtype', 'build_bitnet_linear_layer': 'build a BitLinear module that performs forward pass with packed ternary weights and per-token activation quantization', 'build_replace_with_bitnet_linear': 'replace all nn.Linear layers in a model with BitNet quantized linear layers for inference or training', 'build_bitnet_deserialize': 'deserialize packed BitNet weights from a Hugging Face checkpoint by unpacking and converting tensor formats'}
```
